in writing. − i voted in favour of this recommendation by the european parliament because i support the draft council decision on the participation of switzerland in the 'youth in action' and 'lifelong learning' programmes. although switzerland has opted not to join the european economic area, it cooperates closely with the european union in the fields of education, training and youth. this agreement is appropriate as switzerland commits to the conditions and provisions laid down by the european commission. in addition, projects and initiatives submitted by the swiss side will be subject to the same conditions, rules and procedures as those applied to projects submitted by member states. switzerland shall comply with the financial control and audit provisions of the european union. under the bilateral agreement, switzerland will have to set up a national agency to cooperate on the implementation of the programmes at national level and to contribute financially to each programme on an annual basis (in 2011 €1.7 million for the youth programme and €14.2 million for the lifelong learning programme).
in writing. − i voted in favour of this 2009 report on the situation of human rights in the world and in the european union, as it examines, evaluates and, in specific cases, analyses the european union's activities in the field of human rights and democracy and sets out its future objectives. on violence against women, parliament expressed its deep concern at the prevalence of gender-based discrimination and domestic violence in some countries, and noted that women living in rural areas were a particularly vulnerable group. i therefore support parliament's demand that all human rights dialogues openly raise women's rights issues, in particular as regards the fight against and elimination of all forms of discrimination and violence against women and girls, all forms of harmful traditional practices or customs such as female genital mutilation, early or forced marriage, all forms of trafficking in human beings, domestic violence and the killing of women, exploitation at the workplace and economic exploitation. as millions of children continue to be victims of rape, domestic violence and physical, emotional and sexual violence, i agree that the european union must urgently take additional measures to tackle child labour and to apply existing measures more effectively.
in writing. − although more than 60 years have passed since the universal declaration of human rights was signed, there are still many places in the world where the declaration is a mere sheet of paper. unfortunately, there are many such places in europe, the continent that gave birth to the protection of human rights as a principle of democracy. the european union, which we can confidently call the global leader in the protection of human rights, is working hard to ensure that at least minimum standards of human rights protection are also guaranteed elsewhere in the world. i therefore congratulate my colleague, mrs laima andrikien, on her truly comprehensive report, which has drawn attention to the complex situation in the world. i hope that this report will help to improve the situation and that in future such reports will be shorter.
in writing. − i voted for this year's ep report on human rights, as it covers countries both geographically close to the eu and far away. in the report, the ep strongly urges china, the world's largest executioner, to make public the number of executions carried out in the country, in order to allow for a transparent analysis and debate on the subject. this should make this country move faster to abandon this barbaric form of punishment. on the other hand, it's a shame that this punishment is still used in the democratic united states. the report expresses concern about children involved in armed conflict, who suffer from it or are even forced to take an active part in it. in my amendments to this report, adopted by the ep's foreign affairs committee, i urge the commission and the council to step up their efforts to ensure that the eu guidelines on the involvement of children in armed conflict are complied with in their relations with third countries. i also call on the eu and un institutions to work towards the immediate disarmament, rehabilitation and reintegration of children actively engaged in armed conflict. i call on the north korean authorities to take concrete and tangible measures to improve the human rights situation. invites pyongyang to allow independent international experts to inspect all types of detention facilities and to allow un special rapporteurs to enter the country. i also call on north korea to lift the ban on international staff monitoring aid distribution and to ensure that international aid is delivered to those who need it most.
in writing. − i voted in favour of this resolution and supported a pragmatic approach towards afghanistan and the new counter-insurgency policy which is geared to protecting the local population and rebuilding areas where security has been ensured. the new counter-insurgency policy must be given time to show results, there should be a political solution and negotiations with all sides should take place. i am convinced that any long-term solution must include concrete measures for the eradication of poverty, under-development and discrimination against women, enhancing respect for human rights and the rule of law, strengthening reconciliation mechanisms, ensuring an end to opium production, engaging in a robust state-building exercise, and fully integrating afghanistan into the international community, as well as banishing al-qa’ida from the country. we have to pay tribute to the servicemen and women of all the allied forces who have lost their lives in defending freedom, and to express condolences to their families, as well as to the families of all innocent afghan victims.
in writing. − i voted in favour of this resolution because i believe that this document strengthens the role of the european parliament in its relations with the commission. i share the view of the committee on foreign affairs that it is essential, in order for parliament to be able to exercise its prerogatives fully and in a timely manner, to encourage the commission to actively inform parliament of all draft measures, amended draft measures and final draft measures which it intends to adopt pursuant to article 291 of the treaty on the functioning of the european union, which provides that where legally binding union acts are to be implemented under uniform conditions and implementing powers are to be conferred on the commission, the control of the exercise of such implementing powers by the commission shall be exercised solely by the member states. i believe that, in view of the specificity and political sensitivity of the implementing acts to be adopted under the external financial assistance instruments and in the light of the experience gained in the democratic monitoring dialogue, parliament should be given the opportunity to contribute to this process of defining the content of the draft implementing acts to be adopted under the external financial assistance instruments.
in writing. − i voted in favour of this resolution because the financial stability of the euro requires a permanent crisis management mechanism. the european stability mechanism and/or the european monetary fund should be based on solidarity, should be subject to strict conditionality rules and should be financed, inter alia, by fines imposed on member states following the excessive deficit, excessive debt or excessive imbalance procedure. i also think it is important that the financing of the permanent crisis management mechanism should be based on the 'polluter pays' principle, i.e. member states that are more at risk of their deficit and debt burden should allocate a higher share of the overall funds. these sanctions would be one way of avoiding the kind of financial and economic crisis in the euro area countries that started in greece a few months ago and is still going on.
in writing. − this year saw major decisions to establish the eu's economic governance and financial supervision framework. the eu will establish a european systemic risk board, which will be responsible for overseeing the macroprudential risk management of the financial system in order to avoid periods of severe financial distress, contribute to the smooth functioning of the market and ensure the sustainable contribution of the financial sector to the growth of the eu economy. i support the proposal to create a permanent european stability mechanism and thus further strengthen the economic governance framework to ensure effective and thorough economic surveillance and coordination with a strong focus on prevention. i agree with the proposal in the resolution that non-euro area member states should also be given the opportunity to participate in the establishment of this mechanism and to receive financial assistance when necessary.
in writing. − i voted in favour of this european parliament resolution which discusses the need for member states to establish a permanent crisis management mechanism to ensure financial stability throughout the euro area. this measure aims at ensuring effective and thorough economic surveillance and coordination, with a focus on prevention and a significantly reduced likelihood of future recurrence of a crisis. in order to increase the efficiency of current economic policy coordination processes and to eliminate duplication and to ensure that the european union strategy is understood by market players and citizens, a shift to more integrated strategies and a change in the decision-making process is necessary. i agree that the european parliament's participation in the legislative procedures relating to the crisis management mechanism should be strengthened, thereby improving democratic accountability and taking advantage of the commission's qualifications, independence and impartiality. as the european stability mechanism is intended to complement the new enhanced economic governance framework, parliament calls on the commission, after consulting the european central bank, to present a communication detailing this permanent crisis management mechanism.
in writing. − i supported the motions for resolutions on the need to establish a permanent crisis management mechanism that is reliable, robust, durable and takes account of the most important technical innovations, and that it should be based on the community method, thus ensuring stability in the markets and greater certainty.
in writing. − i voted in favour of this resolution, which calls for the restoration of democratic governance in côte d'ivoire following the presidential elections held on 28 november 2010. the decision of the illegitimate constitutional council of côte d'ivoire, whose members were all appointed by the president at the end of his term, to change the results announced by the electoral commission in violation of the law that the constitutional council is obliged to implement, is contrary to the will of the people of côte d'ivoire as expressed in their votes in the elections. this decision must be reversed, otherwise the country will not emerge from the current post-election political impasse and the reported violence will increase. therefore, for the welfare of the people of côte d'ivoire and for peace in the country, i support the appeal to mr gbagbo to step down and hand over power to mr ouattara, to whom the voters have given their votes of confidence.
